## Refresh

```bash
npm run site:ranking-opportunities
npm run site:ranking-opportunities -- --fixture
npm run site:crm-keywords   # companion full keyword inventory
```

Re-run after SERP / benchmark / CQ / map refresh. Do not treat old opportunity snapshots as current.
